# Exercise library import report: M14 sources

Generated by `npm run library:report` (SPEC-v11 M14 Phase A, sources and process approved by Payam on 2026-10-16).
Names and facts are taken from every source; descriptions, instructions and media only where the licence allows
(instructions are our own). Existing exercise ids never change; a duplicate is not added, nothing is merged.

## Per source

| Source | Commit | Licence | Records | Not imported automatically | Exact duplicates | Reviewed: added | Reviewed: excluded | Reviewed: duplicates |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| wger | `8f72cb2` | Per record: CC-BY-SA 3.0, CC-BY-SA 4.0 or CC0 | 872 | 233 | 133 | 145 | 79 | 282 |
| everkinetic | `446bb9a` | CC-BY-SA 4.0 | 289 | 24 | 182 | 15 | 5 | 63 |
| ExerciseDB | `7455efa` | Names and facts only (media © Gym visual, not used) | 1324 | 224 | 342 | 229 | 91 | 438 |
| Liftosaur | `a7a402c` | Names and facts only (app code AGPL-3.0, not used) | 402 | 61 | 219 | 34 | 16 | 72 |
| exercemus | `0ee5b74` | Names and facts only | 872 | 271 | 597 | 0 | 0 | 4 |

Rows added from the M14 sources: **423**. Library after the import: **1204** exercises (781 before).

### Same picture twice: one image kept (every source, seed/library/duplicate-images.json) (7)

- One Arm Against Wall (`fedb-One_Arm_Against_Wall`): kept `One_Arm_Against_Wall/0.jpg`, left out `One_Arm_Against_Wall/1.jpg` (the same file twice)
- Reverse Grip Bent-Over Rows (`fedb-Reverse_Grip_Bent-Over_Rows`): kept `Reverse_Grip_Bent-Over_Rows/0.jpg`, left out `Reverse_Grip_Bent-Over_Rows/1.jpg` (the same file twice)
- Seated Floor Hamstring Stretch (`fedb-Seated_Floor_Hamstring_Stretch`): kept `Seated_Floor_Hamstring_Stretch/0.jpg`, left out `Seated_Floor_Hamstring_Stretch/1.jpg` (the same file twice)
- Standing Hamstring and Calf Stretch (`fedb-Standing_Hamstring_and_Calf_Stretch`): kept `Standing_Hamstring_and_Calf_Stretch/0.jpg`, left out `Standing_Hamstring_and_Calf_Stretch/1.jpg` (the same file twice)
- The Straddle (`fedb-The_Straddle`): kept `The_Straddle/0.jpg`, left out `The_Straddle/1.jpg` (the same file twice)
- Dumbbell Goblet Squat (`wger-203`): kept `203/1c052351-2af0-4227-aeb0-244008e4b0a8.jpeg`, left out `203/2ab30113-4e08-4d39-9d23-d901ce2c0971.jpeg` (the same file twice)
- Pistol Squat (`wger-456`): kept `456/3b681e59-377b-40db-9113-ca5873ce084b.jpg`, left out `456/93716d06-c378-49b8-820a-8bf3e1d7e45c.jpg` (the same file twice)

- Seated Hip Adduction (`wger:12`) = `fedb-Thigh_Adductor`: Seated machine hip adduction is the library's Thigh Adductor machine.

- Barbell Lying Bicep Curl (`liftosaur:lyingBicepCurl:barbell`) = `fedb-Lying_High_Bench_Barbell_Curl`: A lying barbell biceps curl is the prone high-bench curl already in the library.
- EZ-Bar Lying Bicep Curl (`liftosaur:lyingBicepCurl:ezbar`) = `fedb-Lying_High_Bench_Barbell_Curl`: Same prone high-bench curl; an EZ-bar instead of a straight bar does not make a separate exercise.
- Dumbbell Overhead Press (`liftosaur:overheadPress:dumbbell`) = `fedb-Standing_Dumbbell_Press`: Standing dumbbell overhead press is the library's Standing Dumbbell Press.
- EZ-Bar Overhead Press (`liftosaur:overheadPress:ezbar`) = `ohp`: Same standing overhead press; using an EZ-bar instead of a straight bar is not a material change.
- EZ-Bar Preacher Curl (`liftosaur:preacherCurl:ezbar`) = `fedb-Preacher_Curl`: The library's Preacher Curl is the EZ-bar preacher curl.
- Dumbbell Reverse Curl (`liftosaur:reverseCurl:dumbbell`) = `fedb-Standing_Dumbbell_Reverse_Curl`: Same dumbbell reverse curl; library name only adds 'Standing'.
- Dumbbell Reverse Grip Concentration Curl (`liftosaur:reverseGripConcentrationCurl:dumbbell`) = `edb-0403`: Same seated reverse-grip dumbbell concentration curl as candidate edb-0403.
- Reverse Hyperextension (`liftosaur:reverseHyperextension:bodyweight`) = `fedb-Reverse_Hyperextension`: Same reverse hyperextension; doing it unloaded does not make a separate exercise.
- Cable Reverse Lat Pulldown (`liftosaur:reverseLatPulldown:cable`) = `manual-reverse-grip-lat-pulldown`: Reverse lat pulldown is the underhand-grip pulldown already in the library.
- Barbell Reverse Lunge (`liftosaur:reverseLunge:barbell`) = `wger-1830`: Same barbell reverse lunge as candidate wger-1830 (Barbell Step Back Lunge).
- Reverse Lunge (`liftosaur:reverseLunge:bodyweight`) = `wger-1102`: Same bodyweight reverse lunge as candidate wger-1102 (Alternate back lunges).
- Dumbbell Reverse Lunge (`liftosaur:reverseLunge:dumbbell`) = `fedb-Dumbbell_Rear_Lunge`: Reverse lunge and rear lunge are the same exercise with dumbbells.
- Ring Ab Rollout (`liftosaur:ringAbRollout:bodyweight`) = `fedb-Suspended_Fallout`: A full ring rollout from the feet is the library's Suspended Fallout; rings versus straps is not material.
- Ring Row (`liftosaur:ringRow:bodyweight`) = `fedb-Inverted_Row_with_Straps`: Ring row is the suspended inverted row already in the library; rings versus straps is not material.
- Cable Rowing (`liftosaur:rowing:cable`) = `fedb-Rowing_Stationary`: This is the rowing machine (ergometer), already in the library as Rowing, Stationary.
- Dumbbell Russian Twist (`liftosaur:russianTwist:dumbbell`) = `fedb-Russian_Twist`: Same Russian twist; holding a dumbbell only adds load.
- Machine Seated Leg Press (`liftosaur:seatedLegPress:leverageMachine`) = `leg_press`: Seated leg press is the standard Leg Press already in the library.
- Dumbbell Shoulder Press Parallel Grip (`liftosaur:shoulderPressParallelGrip:dumbbell`) = `edb-0404`: Same seated neutral-grip dumbbell shoulder press as candidate edb-0404.
- Side Hip Abductor (`liftosaur:sideHipAbductor:bodyweight`) = `edb-0710`: Same side-lying hip abduction as candidate edb-0710 (Side Hip Abduction).
- Machine Side Hip Abductor (`liftosaur:sideHipAbductor:leverageMachine`) = `fedb-Thigh_Abductor`: Machine hip abduction is already in the library as Thigh Abductor.
- Side Lying Clam (`liftosaur:sideLyingClam:bodyweight`) = `wger-1823`: Same side-lying clam as candidate wger-1823 (Clamshell).
- Single Leg Bridge (`liftosaur:singleLegBridge:bodyweight`) = `fedb-Single_Leg_Glute_Bridge`: Same exercise as Single Leg Glute Bridge.
- Cable Single Leg Calf Raise (`liftosaur:singleLegCalfRaise:cable`) = `edb-1376`: Same exercise as candidate edb-1376 (Cable Standing One Leg Calf Raise).
- Single Leg Deadlift (`liftosaur:singleLegDeadlift:bodyweight`) = `wger-1388`: Same bodyweight single-leg Romanian deadlift as candidate wger-1388.
- Single Leg Glute Bridge Bent Knee (`liftosaur:singleLegGluteBridgeBentKnee:bodyweight`) = `fedb-Single_Leg_Glute_Bridge`: Only the free leg's position differs; same single-leg glute bridge.
- Single Leg Glute Bridge Straight Leg (`liftosaur:singleLegGluteBridgeStraight:bodyweight`) = `fedb-Single_Leg_Glute_Bridge`: Only the free leg's position differs; same single-leg glute bridge.
- Single Leg Hip Thrust (`liftosaur:singleLegHipThrust:bodyweight`) = `wger-1913`: Same bodyweight single-leg hip thrust as candidate wger-1913 (Unilateral Hip Thrust).
- Dumbbell Snatch (`liftosaur:snatch:dumbbell`) = `edb-3888`: A dumbbell snatch is done one arm at a time; same as candidate edb-3888 (Dumbbell One Arm Snatch).
- Cable Standing Row (`liftosaur:standingRow:cable`) = `edb-0234`: Same standing cable row as candidate edb-0234.
- Cable Standing Row Close Grip (`liftosaur:standingRowCloseGrip:cable`) = `edb-0234`: Close-grip standing cable row is the V-bar standing row in candidate edb-0234.
- Cable Standing Row Rear Delt, Horizontal, With Rope (`liftosaur:standingRowRearHorizontalDeltWithRope:cable`) = `edb-0233`: Same standing rope rear-delt row as candidate edb-0233.
- Barbell Stiff Leg Deadlift (`liftosaur:stiffLegDeadlift:barbell`) = `fedb-Stiff-Legged_Barbell_Deadlift`: Same exercise as Stiff-Legged Barbell Deadlift.
- Dumbbell Superman (`liftosaur:superman:dumbbell`) = `fedb-Superman`: Same superman; holding light dumbbells only adds load.
- Support Hold (`liftosaur:supportHold:bodyweight`) = `wger-1808`: Same top-of-dip support hold as candidate wger-1808 (Parallel Bar Hold).
- Toes To Bar (`liftosaur:toesToBar:bodyweight`) = `fedb-Hanging_Pike`: Lifting straight legs from a hang until the feet reach the bar is the library's Hanging Pike.
- Cable Torso Rotation (`liftosaur:torsoRotation:cable`) = `fedb-Cable_Russian_Twists`: Standing horizontal cable torso rotation is the library's Cable Russian Twists.
- Vertical Row (`liftosaur:verticalRow:bodyweight`) = `fedb-Inverted_Row`: A steeper-angle regression of the inverted row, not a separate exercise.
- V Up (`liftosaur:vUp:bodyweight`) = `fedb-Jackknife_Sit-Up`: A V-up is the library's Jackknife Sit-Up.
- Dumbbell V Up (`liftosaur:vUp:dumbbell`) = `fedb-Jackknife_Sit-Up`: Same V-up (Jackknife Sit-Up); holding a dumbbell only adds load.
- Wrist Roller (`liftosaur:wristRoller:bodyweight`) = `fedb-Wrist_Roller`: Same exercise as Wrist Roller.
- Side Lateral Raise (`exercemus:side-lateral-raise`) = `lateral`: Standing dumbbell side lateral raise is the library's Dumbbell Lateral Raise.
- Standing Military Press (`exercemus:standing-military-press`) = `ohp`: Standing barbell military press is the library's Standing Overhead Press.
- Triceps Pushdown - Rope Attachment (`exercemus:triceps-pushdown-rope-attachment`) = `rope_pushdown`: Same exercise as Triceps Rope Pushdown.
- Weighted Bench Dip (`exercemus:weighted-bench-dip`) = `fedb-Weighted_Bench_Dip`: Same exercise as Weighted Bench Dip.

### Reviewed as different and added or decided on their own (dedupe-review.json: different) (225)

- Braced Squat (`wger:124`) ≠ Bodyweight Squat (`fedb-Bodyweight_Squat`): Unlike the bodyweight squat, a plate is held out at arm's length, which loads the front of the body and forces a braced torso.
- Dumbbell Goblet Squat (`wger:203`) ≠ Dumbbell Squat (`fedb-Dumbbell_Squat`): Closest Dumbbell Squat holds two dumbbells at the sides; the goblet squat holds one dumbbell at the chest, giving a more upright torso. Only the heel-elevated goblet variant exists.
- Hindu Squats (`wger:291`) ≠ Bodyweight Squat (`fedb-Bodyweight_Squat`): Unlike the bodyweight squat, the heels rise at the bottom and the arms swing in a continuous rhythm.
- Diamond push ups (`wger:386`) ≠ Pushups (`fedb-Pushups`): Hand variant: hands together forming a diamond under the chest, a commonly named triceps-focused push-up distinct from the standard and close-grip versions.
- Pike Push Ups (`wger:454`) ≠ Pushups (`fedb-Pushups`): Unlike the standard push-up, the hips are raised into an inverted V so the press becomes a vertical, shoulder-dominant movement.
- Pistol Squat (`wger:456`) ≠ Bodyweight Squat (`fedb-Bodyweight_Squat`): Single-leg squat with the free leg held straight in front; the closest Bodyweight Squat is two-legged and the library's pistol is the Smith machine version.
- Row (`wger:508`) ≠ Upright Barbell Row (`fedb-Upright_Barbell_Row`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Generic 'Row' with mixed equipment and an encyclopedia-style description; too vague to identify.
- Side to Side Push Ups (`wger:583`) ≠ Push Up to Side Plank (`fedb-Push_Up_to_Side_Plank`): Differs from Push Up to Side Plank: the chest shifts over one hand each rep, with no rotation into a side plank.
- Splinter Sit-ups (`wger:607`) ≠ Sit-Up (`fedb-Sit-Up`): 'Splinter' is a typo of sprinter; unlike a plain Sit-Up it adds a twist, knee drive and running arm swing.
- Squat Thrust (`wger:616`) ≠ Bodyweight Squat (`fedb-Bodyweight_Squat`): Conditioning move with a kick back to plank; not the same as Bodyweight Squat, and no jump or push-up like a burpee.
- Sumo Squats (`wger:632`) ≠ Bodyweight Squat (`fedb-Bodyweight_Squat`): Wide sumo stance with no load; existing sumo and wide-stance squats use a barbell or dumbbell.
- Wall Pushup (`wger:713`) ≠ Pushups (`fedb-Pushups`): Hands on a wall with the body nearly upright; a beginner regression, unlike the floor push-up.
- Wall Squat (`wger:718`) ≠ Bodyweight Squat (`fedb-Bodyweight_Squat`): Isometric hold with the back on a wall; Bodyweight Squat is a dynamic squat.
- commando pull-ups (`wger:980`) ≠ Weighted Pull Ups (`fedb-Weighted_Pull_Ups`): Close staggered grip with the head passing alternate sides of the bar; not a standard or weighted pull-up.
- Hindu Pushups (`wger:1080`) ≠ Pushups (`fedb-Pushups`): Flowing dive from a pike to an arched top position; not a standard push-up.
- Walking (`wger:1104`) ≠ Bodyweight Walking Lunge (`fedb-Bodyweight_Walking_Lunge`): Steady walking outdoors or indoors, not the lunge; the library only has treadmill and trail walking.
- Push-Ups | Parallettes (`wger:1113`) ≠ Pushups (`fedb-Pushups`): Hands raised on parallettes let the chest drop below hand level; deeper range than a floor push-up.
- knee push-ups (`wger:1218`) ≠ Pushups (`fedb-Pushups`): Knees stay on the floor to reduce load; a regression of the standard push-up.
- Dumbbell close grip bench press (`wger:1228`) ≠ Close-Grip Dumbbell Press (`fedb-Close-Grip_Dumbbell_Press`): Two dumbbells squeezed together throughout (squeeze press); the library's Close-Grip Dumbbell Press uses one dumbbell held across the palms.
- Dumbbell Single-leg Hip Thrust (`wger:1234`) ≠ Barbell Hip Thrust (`fedb-Barbell_Hip_Thrust`): Single-leg stance with a dumbbell; the library has only the two-leg barbell hip thrust and a floor single-leg glute bridge. Source equipment (barbell) corrected to dumbbell.
- Front Lever (`wger:1245`) ≠ Front lever tuck (`wger-1214`): Full front lever hold; a harder progression than the tuck front lever. Source equipment (machine) corrected to bodyweight.
- Front lever pull-up (`wger:1252`) ≠ Pull Ups on Machine (`wger-477`): Rows to the bar from a front lever position; not a vertical pull-up like Pull Ups on Machine.
- Schoulder Raise (Dumbbell) (`wger:1338`) ≠ Dumbbell Raise (`fedb-Dumbbell_Raise`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Too vague: a dumbbell 'shoulder raise' could be a lateral raise, front raise or shrug, and the source gives only generic cues.
- box jumps (`wger:1373`) ≠ Front Box Jump (`fedb-Front_Box_Jump`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Box jump is a plyometric jump.
- Upper Back (`wger:1381`) ≠ Upper Back Stretch (`fedb-Upper_Back_Stretch`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Too vague to identify; the name and source text describe no specific movement.
- Standing Pancake Good Morning (`wger:1397`) ≠ Standing Pancake (`wger-1396`): Dynamic wide-stance hinge done for reps, not the static Standing Pancake hold.
- Crossbody Hamstring Stretch (`wger:1400`) ≠ Hamstring Stretch (`fedb-Hamstring_Stretch`): Standing fold with the legs crossed; differs from the lying strap Hamstring Stretch and the standing toe touch.
- Cossack squat (`wger:1407`) ≠ Bodyweight Squat (`fedb-Bodyweight_Squat`): Wide-stance squat shifting onto one leg at a time; not a bilateral squat like Bodyweight Squat.
- Pin Squat (`wger:1437`) ≠ Squats (`wger-615`): Squat with a dead stop on safety pins at a set depth; a named variant of the back squat.
- Pin OHP (`wger:1439`) ≠ Pin Presses (`fedb-Pin_Presses`): Overhead press from pins at chin height; Pin Presses is a bench movement.
- Towel Superman (`wger:1455`) ≠ Superman (`fedb-Superman`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Non-standard home variation of the Superman using a towel prop.
- Cross-Body Cable Y-Raise (`wger:1458`) ≠ Cable Y-Raise (`manual-cable-y-raise`): One arm pulling from a single low pulley across the body; the library Cable Y-Raise is the two-arm crossed-cable version.
- Pause Hack Squats (`wger:1464`) ≠ Hack Squats (`wger-1414`): Named tempo variant with a 1-2 second pause at the bottom, like Pause Squat versus Barbell Back Squat.
- Incline Close Grip Barbell Bench Press (`wger:1467`) ≠ Close-Grip Barbell Bench Press (`fedb-Close-Grip_Barbell_Bench_Press`): Incline bench angle; the library Close-Grip Barbell Bench Press is flat.
- Plank Jacks (`wger:1489`) ≠ Plank (`plank`): Adds jumping-jack leg movement for conditioning; the Plank is a static hold.
- DB Underhand bench press (`wger:1497`) ≠ Dumbbell Bench Press (`fedb-Dumbbell_Bench_Press`): Underhand grip with tucked elbows; the grip makes it different from the Dumbbell Bench Press.
- Handstand (`wger:1516`) ≠ Handstand Push-Ups (`fedb-Handstand_Push-Ups`): Static balance hold with no pressing; differs from Handstand Push-Ups.
- Wide Pull Up (`wger:1542`) ≠ Weighted Pull Ups (`fedb-Weighted_Pull_Ups`): Wide front grip; differs from the standard pull-up and from the library's behind-the-neck wide-grip version.
- Archer Pull Up (`wger:1545`) ≠ Weighted Pull Ups (`fedb-Weighted_Pull_Ups`): Shifts the load onto one arm as a one-arm pull-up progression; differs from standard pull-ups.
- Smith Machine Split Squat (`wger:1593`) ≠ Smith Single-Leg Split Squat (`fedb-Smith_Single-Leg_Split_Squat`): Both feet on the floor; the library Smith Single-Leg Split Squat has the rear foot on a bench.
- Typewriter Pull-ups (`wger:1607`) ≠ Weighted Pull Ups (`fedb-Weighted_Pull_Ups`): Traverses side to side at the top; differs from standard pull-ups and the diagonal Side To Side Chins.
- Bodyweight Biceps Curl (`wger:1608`) ≠ Cable Curls (`wger-1531`): Bodyweight curl under a fixed bar; different equipment from the cable curl.
- Seated Dumbbell Calf Raise (`wger:1620`) ≠ Dumbbell Seated One-Leg Calf Raise (`fedb-Dumbbell_Seated_One-Leg_Calf_Raise`): Both legs at once; the library Dumbbell Seated One-Leg Calf Raise is single-leg.
- Dumbbell Front Squat (`wger:1640`) ≠ Dumbbell Squat (`fedb-Dumbbell_Squat`): Dumbbells racked on the front of the shoulders; the library Dumbbell Squat holds them at the sides.
- Dumbbell Romanian Deadlift (`wger:1652`) ≠ Dumbbell Single-Leg Romanian Deadlift (`manual-dumbbell-single-leg-rdl`): Two-leg version; the closest library match is single-leg, and the library keeps the RDL separate from the stiff-legged deadlift.
- Dumbbell Side Squat (`wger:1653`) ≠ Dumbbell Squat (`fedb-Dumbbell_Squat`): Steps to the side and bends one knee; a lateral lunge, not a bilateral squat.
- Lat Pulldown - Cross Body Single Arm (`wger:1659`) ≠ One Arm Lat Pulldown (`fedb-One_Arm_Lat_Pulldown`): Seated diagonally so the pull crosses the body for a longer lat stretch; differs in body position from One Arm Lat Pulldown.
- Kickstand RDL (`wger:1688`) ≠ Romanian Deadlift (`rdl`): Staggered kickstand stance that loads mostly one leg; a different stance from the standard Romanian Deadlift.
- Side Straight-Arm Pulldown (Cable) (`wger:1727`) ≠ Straight-Arm Pulldown (`fedb-Straight-Arm_Pulldown`): One arm, standing side-on, arm sweeping down beside the body; Straight-Arm Pulldown is a two-arm bar pull in front.
- unilateral cross body cable pull down (`wger:1795`) ≠ Lat Pulldown - Cross Body Single Arm (`wger-1659`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Vague, non-standard straight-arm cable sweep with no clear target or recognisable name.
- Landmine Squat to Press (`wger:1829`) ≠ Landmine Press (`manual-landmine-press`): Adds a full squat before each press; Landmine Press is the press alone and Linear Jammer uses a short dip.
- Butterfly Stretch (`wger:1843`) ≠ Butterfly (`wger-135`): A seated groin stretch, not the Butterfly chest machine. Candidate edb-1494 Butterfly Yoga Pose is the same stretch.
- L-sit (`wger:1852`) ≠ Straddle L-Sit (`wger-1847`): Legs held together in front; Straddle L-Sit spreads them wide. Candidate edb-3419 L-Sit On Floor is the same exercise.
- Side stretch (`wger:1861`) ≠ On Your Side Quad Stretch (`fedb-On_Your_Side_Quad_Stretch`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Video-only record with a vague name; cannot tell which side stretch is meant.
- Cat Plank (`wger:1911`) ≠ Plank (`plank`): Source name Cat Plank; a hover on hands and toes with knees just off the floor, not the forearm Plank.
- Extreme Shoulder Stretch (`wger:1920`) ≠ Shoulder Stretch (`fedb-Shoulder_Stretch`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Vague loaded-stretch concept for the shoulders with no defined setup or equipment.
- Seated Cable chest fly (`wger:1922`) ≠ Cable Chest Fly (`fly`): Seated on an upright bench; the library Cable Chest Fly is standing and Flat Bench Cable Flyes is lying.
- Barbell Silverback Shrug (`wger:1925`) ≠ Barbell Shrug (`fedb-Barbell_Shrug`): Hinged-forward shrug that retracts the shoulder blades; Barbell Shrug is an upright elevation shrug.
- Seated Rear Lateral Cable Raise (`everkinetic:0035`) ≠ Cable Seated Lateral Raise (`fedb-Cable_Seated_Lateral_Raise`): Seated bent-over rear delt raise with crossed cables; the closest match is an upright side lateral raise.
- Dead Lifts: Smith Machine (`everkinetic:0100`) ≠ Smith Machine Romanian Deadlift (`manual-smith-machine-romanian-deadlift`): Conventional pull from low in the Smith machine with knee bend, not the hip-hinge Romanian or stiff-legged version.
- Dead Lifts: Dumbbell (`everkinetic:0107`) ≠ Dumbbell Romanian Deadlift (`wger-1652`): Conventional deadlift from the floor with bent knees; the closest is the Romanian (hinge-only) version.
- Smith Machine Hack Squat (`everkinetic:0126`) ≠ Hack Squat (`fedb-Hack_Squat`): Bar held behind the legs in a Smith machine; differs from the Hack Squat sled machine and the free-barbell version.
- One Arm Snatch with Barbell (`everkinetic:0148`) ≠ Snatch (`fedb-Snatch`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Single-arm barbell snatch is a circus-style, unsafe lift nobody programs; use the dumbbell snatch or barbell snatch instead.
- Kneeling Triceps Concentration Extension with Cable (`everkinetic:0176`) ≠ Kneeling Cable Triceps Extension (`fedb-Kneeling_Cable_Triceps_Extension`): Single-arm high-pulley extension with the upper arm braced on the thigh; the closest is a two-hand overhead rope extension.
- Triceps Extension: Barbell (Seated, Overhead) (`everkinetic:0193`) ≠ Overhead Triceps Extension (`wger-1519`): Seated, back-supported version; the library has only the standing barbell overhead extension, and wger-1519 is a generic sit-or-stand entry.
- Triceps Extension: Dumbbell (Incline) (`everkinetic:0209`) ≠ Dumbbell Triceps Extension (`wger-211`): Lying back on an incline bench with two dumbbells; the closest is a generic seated/standing overhead extension.
- Biceps Curl: Squat with Dumbbell (`everkinetic:0256`) ≠ Dumbbell Biceps Curl (`curl`): Squat combined with a curl; not a plain dumbbell curl.
- Dead Lift: Barbell (Biceps Curl) (`everkinetic:0259`) ≠ Biceps Curls With Barbell (`wger-91`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Gimmicky deadlift-plus-curl combo; the curl caps the load so the hinge is pointless, and it is rarely programmed.
- Assisted Lying Leg Raise With Throw Down (`exercisedb:0013`) ≠ Assisted Lying Leg Raise With Lateral Throw Down (`edb-0012`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Partner throw-down leg raise drill; not a programmable gym exercise.
- Barbell Alternate Biceps Curl (`exercisedb:0023`) ≠ Biceps Curls With Barbell (`wger-91`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Alternating arms is not possible with a single barbell; mislabelled standard barbell curl.
- Barbell Decline Bent Arm Pullover (`exercisedb:0034`) ≠ Bent-Arm Barbell Pullover (`fedb-Bent-Arm_Barbell_Pullover`): Decline bench with bent arms and shoulder-width grip; library has the flat bent-arm and the wide-grip straight-arm decline pullovers (compare later edb-1255).
- Barbell Incline Row (`exercisedb:0049`) ≠ Row (`wger-508`): Chest-supported on an incline bench; differs from the flat-bench Seal Row and the T-bar version (later twin lift-chestSupportedRow:barbell).
- Barbell One Arm Bent Over Row (`exercisedb:0064`) ≠ Barbell Bent-Over Row (`row`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Rowing a full barbell held at its centre with one hand is impractical; single-arm rows exist with dumbbell, landmine and cable.
- Barbell Pullover (`exercisedb:0073`) ≠ Barbell Pullover To Press (`edb-0022`): Arms kept straight; the library has only the bent-arm barbell pullover (matching its straight- and bent-arm dumbbell pair).
- Cable Alternate Triceps Extension (`exercisedb:0149`) ≠ Triceps Extensions on Cable (`wger-659`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Alternating-arm variant only adds noise; single-arm cable triceps extensions are already in the library.
- Cable Decline Fly (`exercisedb:0158`) ≠ Fly With Cable (`wger-237`): Fly on a decline bench; the library cable flies are standing or on a flat or incline bench.
- Cable Reverse Preacher Curl (`exercisedb:0209`) ≠ Cable Preacher Curl (`fedb-Cable_Preacher_Curl`): Overhand grip on the cable; the library has the underhand cable preacher curl and the barbell reverse preacher curl.
- Cable Reverse Wrist Curl (`exercisedb:0210`) ≠ Cable Wrist Curl (`fedb-Cable_Wrist_Curl`): Palms-down grip; the library cable wrist curls are palms-up.
- Cable Seated Wide-Grip Row (`exercisedb:0218`) ≠ Cable Decline Seated Wide-Grip Row (`edb-0159`): Wide overhand bar grip; the library Seated Cable Row uses a close handle.
- Cable Standing Rear Delt Row (With Rope) (`exercisedb:0233`) ≠ Cable Rope Rear-Delt Rows (`fedb-Cable_Rope_Rear-Delt_Rows`): Standing at a chest-height pulley; the library rope rear-delt row is seated at a low-row station, and Face Pull pulls to the face.
- Cable Standing Twist Row (V-Bar) (`exercisedb:0236`) ≠ Cable Standing Row (V-Bar) (`edb-0234`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Gimmicky twisting variant of the standing V-bar row; not a standard programmed lift.
- Cable Supine Reverse Fly (`exercisedb:0240`) ≠ Reverse Cable Flye (`wger-1473`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Name (supine) and source setup (prone) conflict, so the movement cannot be identified with confidence; cable rear-delt flys already covered.
- Cable Twist (`exercisedb:0243`) ≠ Cable Russian Twists (`fedb-Cable_Russian_Twists`): Standing horizontal rotation; Cable Russian Twists is done lying on a ball, wood chops and lifts are diagonal, and wger-672 is seated.
- Decline Sit-Up (`exercisedb:0282`) ≠ Sit-Up (`fedb-Sit-Up`): Full sit-up range on a decline bench; library has the floor Sit-Up and the partial-range Decline Crunch.
- Dumbbell Incline Shrug (`exercisedb:0329`) ≠ Dumbbell Shrug (`fedb-Dumbbell_Shrug`): Chest-supported prone shrug (elevation); differs from the standing Dumbbell Shrug and the retraction-only Middle Back Shrug.
- Dumbbell Rear Fly (`exercisedb:0378`) ≠ Dumbbell Flyes (`fedb-Dumbbell_Flyes`): Standing unsupported bent-over reverse fly; library versions are incline-prone, seated, lying or head-supported, and Dumbbell Flyes is a chest move.
- Dumbbell Reverse Preacher Curl (`exercisedb:0384`) ≠ Dumbbell Preacher Curl (`edb-0372`): Pronated grip on the preacher bench; differs from the supinated dumbbell preacher curl, and the library reverse preacher curl is barbell.
- Dumbbell Seated Front Raise (`exercisedb:0392`) ≠ Dumbbell Seated Alternate Front Raise (`edb-0387`): Seated position; the library dumbbell front raises are all standing.
- Dumbbell Single Leg Calf Raise (`exercisedb:0409`) ≠ Dumbbell Seated One-Leg Calf Raise (`fedb-Dumbbell_Seated_One-Leg_Calf_Raise`): Standing single-leg; the library dumbbell calf raises are two-legged or seated.
- Dumbbell Single Leg Squat (`exercisedb:0411`) ≠ Dumbbell Single Leg Split Squat (`edb-0410`): Free-standing single-leg squat with dumbbells; the split squat keeps the rear foot down and the library pistol squat is Smith-only.
- Ez Barbell Reverse Grip Curl (`exercisedb:0451`) ≠ Reverse EZ Bar Cable Curls (`wger-914`): Free-standing EZ-bar reverse curl; closest is a cable curl, and the library reverse curl uses a straight bar (EZ and straight-bar curls are listed separately).
- Front Plank With Twist (`exercisedb:0464`) ≠ Front Plank (`wger-1307`): Adds a hip rotation for the obliques; a static front plank has no twist.
- Hanging Straight Twisting Leg Hip Raise (`exercisedb:0476`) ≠ Hanging Straight Leg Hip Raise (`edb-0474`): Adds a rotation to each side; differs from the straight hanging leg raise.
- Lever Front Pulldown (`exercisedb:0579`) ≠ Front Lever (`wger-1245`): Lever machine pulldown; closest match (Front Lever) is an unrelated gymnastics hold, and the library Lat Pulldown is cable.
- Lever Kneeling Leg Curl (`exercisedb:0582`) ≠ Leg Curl (`wger-364`): Kneeling single-leg machine; the library has lying, seated and standing leg curls.
- Lever One Arm Shoulder Press (`exercisedb:0590`) ≠ Leverage Shoulder Press (`fedb-Leverage_Shoulder_Press`): One arm at a time; the Leverage Shoulder Press is two-arm.
- March Sit (Wall) (`exercisedb:0624`) ≠ Wall-sit (`wger-1408`): Adds alternating foot lifts to the wall sit, loading one leg at a time.
- One Arm Dip (`exercisedb:0639`) ≠ Dips (`wger-194`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Single-arm bench dip is an awkward variant rarely programmed; bench dips are already in the library.
- Seated Leg Raise (`exercisedb:0689`) ≠ Leg Raise (`wger-376`): Seated straight-leg raise; differs from the lying leg raise (position) and seated leg tucks (bent knees).
- Smith Reverse-Grip Press (`exercisedb:0764`) ≠ Smith Decline Reverse-Grip Press (`edb-0754`): Flat bench; the closest match is the decline version.
- Spider Crawl Push Up (`exercisedb:0778`) ≠ Spider Crawl (`fedb-Spider_Crawl`): Full push-up with a knee-to-elbow drive each rep; the library Spider Crawl holds the bottom position without pressing.
- Superman Push-Up (`exercisedb:0803`) ≠ Pushups (`fedb-Pushups`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Explosive push-up with a full-body flight phase; a plyometric/circus variant.
- Triceps Press (`exercisedb:0816`) ≠ Seated Triceps Press (`wger-1749`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Generic name; the bodyweight movement it denotes cannot be identified with confidence.
- Weighted Standing Curl (`exercisedb:0853`) ≠ Leg Curls (standing) (`wger-367`): Standing biceps curl with a weight plate; library only has the reverse-grip plate curl. Plate equipment follows the library's Reverse Plate Curls.

- Lying Leg-Hip Raise (`exercisedb:0865`) ≠ Leg Raises, Lying (`wger-377`): Adds a straight-leg hip lift at the top; plain lying leg raise stops at vertical and Bent-Knee Hip Raise uses bent knees.

- Cable Decline Press (`exercisedb:1261`) ≠ Cable Decline One Arm Press (`edb-1260`): Two-arm high-to-low cable press; the single-arm version is a separate exercise and the library only has flat and incline cable presses.
- Drop Push Up (`exercisedb:1275`) ≠ Pushups (`fedb-Pushups`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Drop push-up is a plyometric depth drill.
- Dumbbell Decline One Arm Fly (`exercisedb:1276`) ≠ Decline Dumbbell Flyes (`fedb-Decline_Dumbbell_Flyes`): Single-arm version; the library Decline Dumbbell Flyes uses both arms.
- Dumbbell Incline One Arm Fly (`exercisedb:1279`) ≠ Incline Dumbbell Flyes (`fedb-Incline_Dumbbell_Flyes`): Single-arm version; the library Incline Dumbbell Flyes uses both arms.

- Cable Standing One Leg Calf Raise (`exercisedb:1376`) ≠ Cable Standing Calf Raise (`edb-1375`): Kept as the one single-leg cable calf raise row (Liftosaur's Cable Single Leg Calf Raise points here); the library has the two-leg cable calf raise only.

- Bodyweight Squatting Row (`exercisedb:3168`) ≠ Bodyweight Squatting Row (With Towel) (`edb-3167`): Post row performed from a deep squat, changing body position and pull angle versus the standing Bodyweight Mid Row; the towel copy is excluded.

- Machine Pistol Squat (`liftosaur:pistolSquat:leverageMachine`) ≠ Smith Machine Pistol Squat (`fedb-Smith_Machine_Pistol_Squat`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Not a real machine exercise; equipment permutation of the pistol squat (Smith Machine Pistol Squat already exists).

- Shrimp Squat (`liftosaur:shrimpSquat:bodyweight`) ≠ Bodyweight Squat (`fedb-Bodyweight_Squat`): Single-leg squat with the rear foot held behind; a different exercise from the two-legged Bodyweight Squat.

- Machine Single Leg Hip Thrust (`liftosaur:singleLegHipThrust:leverageMachine`) ≠ Machine Hip Thrust (`lift-hipThrust-leverageMachine`): One-leg version on a hip thrust machine; Machine Hip Thrust is two-legged.
- Machine Squat (`liftosaur:squat:leverageMachine`) ≠ Chair Squat (`fedb-Chair_Squat`): not a duplicate; excluded on review: Too generic to identify which machine; specific squat machines (hack, V-squat, pendulum, Smith) are already in the library.
- Barbell Sumo Deadlift High Pull (`liftosaur:sumoDeadliftHighPull:barbell`) ≠ Barbell Deadlift High Pull (`lift-deadliftHighPull-barbell`): Wide stance with a narrow grip; differs from the conventional-stance Deadlift High Pull.
- Tuck Front Lever Row (`liftosaur:tuckFrontLeverRow:bodyweight`) ≠ Front Lever Row (`lift-frontLeverRow-bodyweight`): Tucked-knee regression of the front lever row with much easier leverage.
- Wall Handstand (`liftosaur:wallHandstand:bodyweight`) ≠ Handstand (`wger-1516`): Wall-supported static hold; differs from a freestanding handstand and from Handstand Push-Ups.

### wger: excluded on review (79)

- Abdominal Stabilization: Vague name with no real description (German note says lying on a roller); cannot identify, and foam roller work is excluded.
- Full Sit Outs: Plank kick-through drill from wrestling or animal-flow training, not a programmable gym exercise.
- Hercules Pillars: Strongman-style isometric hold between two cable stacks; novelty, not a standard gym exercise.
- High Knee Jumps: Tuck jump; plyometric jumps are not imported.
- Incline Plank With Alternate Floor Touch: Plank with the feet on a gym ball; stability balls are excluded.
- Prone Scapular Retraction - Arms at Side: Low-load physio activation drill with an unclear arm position; not a gym exercise.
- Row: Generic 'Row' with mixed equipment and an encyclopedia-style description; too vague to identify.
- Upper External Oblique: Named after a muscle; the source gives no identifiable movement.
- Wall Slides: Shoulder mobility and warm-up drill, not a training exercise.
- LYING DUMBBELL ROW SS SEATED SHRUG: A superset of two exercises (dumbbell row plus shrug), not a single exercise.
- Suspended crossess: Needs TRX/suspension straps.
- Bag training: Boxing heavy-bag work is sport training and too vague to log as one exercise.
- 3D lunge warmup: Warm-up drill.
- Side Slides + Squats: Agility drill combining lateral shuffles with squats.
- Wall Drills: Vague lateral wall drills for rehab or sport, not one identifiable exercise.
- Claps over the head: Overhead clapping is a warm-up drill, not a training exercise.
- Yoga exercise: Cow-cat: Cat-cow is a dynamic yoga mobility flow; the static Cat Stretch is already in the library.
- Talons fesses: French name for butt kicks, a running warm-up drill.
- High Knee Skips HD: High-knee skipping is a running warm-up drill.
- Lateral Push Off: Hopping laterally from leg to leg is a plyometric agility drill.
- Schoulder Raise (Dumbbell): Too vague: a dumbbell 'shoulder raise' could be a lateral raise, front raise or shrug, and the source gives only generic cues.
- box jumps: Box jump is a plyometric jump.
- TORSO TWIST: Dynamic standing torso twist is a warm-up mobility drill.
- Upper Back: Too vague to identify; the name and source text describe no specific movement.
- Hamstring Kicks: Straight-leg kicks are a dynamic warm-up drill.
- Elephant Walks: Elephant walks are a dynamic hamstring warm-up drill.
- Crossbody Leg Swings: Leg swings are a dynamic warm-up drill.
- Hamstring Chokes: Dynamic hamstring mobility drill (bend and straighten the leg), similar to the library's 90/90 Hamstring.
- Reverse Wood Chops: Performed with a resistance band (excluded equipment); the cable version is already in the library as Standing Cable Lift.
- Lat Pull DB: Unclear, non-standard dumbbell movement; the name and cues do not identify a recognisable exercise.
- Kreis Press DB: Obscure, non-standard combination press with an unrecognised name.
- Towel Superman: Non-standard home variation of the Superman using a towel prop.
- punches: Shadow-boxing punches; a boxing drill, not a gym exercise.
- W-Raise: Source describes a gimmicky leg-drawing reverse crunch, not the shoulder W-raise; not a standard exercise.
- Black Widow Knee Slides: Obscure branded core move; essentially a cross-body plank knee drive covered by Mountain Climbers and Spider Crawl.
- Seated Corkscrew: Obscure seated knee-tuck twist with a gimmick name; not a standard gym exercise.
- Bizeps Curls Trifecta: A set combining three different curls, not a single exercise.
- Omni Cable Cross-over: Vague crossover with one cable high and one low; no established exercise behind the name.
- Rocking Triceps Pushdown: Gimmicky whole-body rocking version of the rope pushdown.
- Hyper Y W Combo: Combination of two raises on a hyperextension bench or stability ball; not a single exercise.
- DB Upper Chest Variation: Vague curl-like dumbbell movement with no recognisable name.
- DB UCV: Unclear abbreviation for a vague curl-like dumbbell movement.
- Dumbbell Bradford press: Awkward dumbbell adaptation of the Bradford press that is rarely programmed; the barbell versions are already in the library.
- Kong Curl: Obscure alternating pronated cable curl with a gimmick name; no established exercise.
- Drop Curl: Vague alternating partial dumbbell curl; not an established exercise.
- Lateral Walk: Squat-position side steps; a band or warm-up drill.
- Sled Push: Sled push; sleds are excluded.
- Battle Ropes: Battle ropes are excluded.
- Ball Slams: Needs a medicine or slam ball.
- Delt Stretch: Too vague: no position or direction for the deltoid stretch.
- Snap Down: Landing and plyometric drill.
- Hip hinge: A movement-pattern drill rather than an exercise; loaded hinges are already covered (Good Morning, Romanian Deadlift).
- Bronco: Rugby shuttle-run fitness test, not a gym exercise.
- Trap press: Misnamed: describes a supine scapular protraction (serratus punch), a niche rehab drill with no matching muscle key.
- March or jog in place: Marching or jogging in place is a warm-up drill.
- Sliding Lateral Lunge: Source version uses a kettlebell and a slider disc; a minor variant of the lateral lunge.
- Arm Raises (T/Y/I): Combination of three different raises in one entry, not a single exercise.
- Modified pulldown: Vague 'modified' close-grip pulldown with an unclear bench setup.
- Cable Triceps Press: Unclear single-arm cable triceps press with no established name; pushdown and extension variants already cover it.
- Curl  - With Shoulder Elevated: Vague curl with the elbow raised to shoulder level; no clear setup or established name.
- Leg Swings (Front–Back): Dynamic leg swings are a warm-up drill.
- Incline Static Hold: An isometric hold at mid-range of the incline dumbbell press; a set technique, not a distinct exercise.
- Shoulder Dumbbell Pendular Exercise: Codman pendulum swing is a passive shoulder rehab drill, not a training exercise.
- Neck extension: Mislabeled record: named Neck Extension with SZ-bar equipment but describes a single-arm high-pulley triceps extension.
- Isometric Squat to Failure: A set technique (static squat held to failure); the isometric squat is covered by the Wall-sit candidate.
- unilateral cross body cable pull down: Vague, non-standard straight-arm cable sweep with no clear target or recognisable name.
- ankle dorsiflexion rocks: Ankle dorsiflexion rocks are a dynamic mobility warm-up drill.
- External Rotation Stretch: Mislabeled record: named a stretch but describes a knee-supported external rotation with mixed barbell or dumbbell equipment.
- Bent-Leg Hamstring Stretch: Video-only record; the bent-leg variant cannot be identified and the library already has several hamstring stretches.
- Horse Stance (Side Splits): Confused record: the name mixes a horse-stance hold and side splits, and the description is a paused bodyweight wide squat.
- L-Sit (Foot Supported): Feet-supported regression of the L-Sit; a near-identical variant that only adds noise.
- Side stretch: Video-only record with a vague name; cannot tell which side stretch is meant.
- Lunge with Twist Stretch: Video-only lunge-and-twist warm-up drill; World's Greatest Stretch already covers this pattern.
- Supine Hip Abduction: Low-load supine leg-slide rehab drill; hip abduction is covered by the side-lying, cable and machine options.
- Pullback: Vague description of a bent-over cable pull with back extension; cannot be identified.
- Leg Wheel: Non-standard name for a small supine knee-circle drill; not a recognisable gym exercise.
- Extreme Pec Stretch: Vague loaded-stretch concept for the chest with no defined setup or equipment.
- Extreme Shoulder Stretch: Vague loaded-stretch concept for the shoulders with no defined setup or equipment.
- Extreme Lat Stretch: Vague loaded-stretch concept for the lats with no defined setup or equipment.

### everkinetic: excluded on review (5)

- One Arm Snatch with Barbell: Single-arm barbell snatch is a circus-style, unsafe lift nobody programs; use the dumbbell snatch or barbell snatch instead.
- One Arm Bicep Curl with Olympic Bar or Barbell: Single-arm curl balancing a full barbell by its middle is a gimmicky balance stunt, not a programmable curl.
- Dead Lift: Barbell (Biceps Curl): Gimmicky deadlift-plus-curl combo; the curl caps the load so the hinge is pointless, and it is rarely programmed.
- Step Up: Dumbbell (Bicep Curl, Single Leg Balance): Gimmicky triple combo (step-up, curl and single-leg balance); the component exercises cover it.
- Stork Stance Bicep Curl with Dumbbells: Gimmicky balance variant of the dumbbell curl (curling on one leg); adds nothing a gym would program.

### ExerciseDB: excluded on review (91)

- Assisted Hanging Knee Raise With Throw Down: Partner throw-down drill with an explosive leg swing; not a gym exercise worth programming.
- Assisted Lying Leg Raise With Lateral Throw Down: Partner throw-down leg raise drill; not a programmable gym exercise.
- Assisted Lying Leg Raise With Throw Down: Partner throw-down leg raise drill; not a programmable gym exercise.
- Barbell Alternate Biceps Curl: Alternating arms is not possible with a single barbell; mislabelled standard barbell curl.
- Barbell One Arm Bent Over Row: Rowing a full barbell held at its centre with one hand is impractical; single-arm rows exist with dumbbell, landmine and cable.
- Barbell Rear Delt Raise: A straight-arm rear-delt raise is not workable with a fixed bar; barbell rear-delt work is covered by Barbell Rear Delt Row.
- Barbell Skier: Unclear jumping barbell 'skier' move; not a recognisable gym exercise.
- Cable Alternate Triceps Extension: Alternating-arm variant only adds noise; single-arm cable triceps extensions are already in the library.
- Cable Rear Drive: Obscure cable 'rear drive' with no clear, recognisable technique; cable kickbacks cover the movement.
- Cable Standing Twist Row (V-Bar): Gimmicky twisting variant of the standing V-bar row; not a standard programmed lift.
- Cable Supine Reverse Fly: Name (supine) and source setup (prone) conflict, so the movement cannot be identified with confidence; cable rear-delt flys already covered.
- Cable Twisting Pull: Vague 'twisting pull'; unclear, non-standard rotational row variant.
- Dumbbell Seated One Arm Rotate: Ambiguous: unclear whether it is a forearm rotation or a shoulder external rotation; both are already in the library.
- Kick Out Sit: Animal-flow style kick-through drill; not a recognisable programmable gym exercise.
- Monster Walk: Monster walk depends on a resistance band (excluded equipment); without it it is a warm-up drill.
- Olympic Barbell Hammer Curl: Hammer (neutral) grip is not possible on a straight Olympic barbell; movement cannot be identified.
- One Arm Dip: Single-arm bench dip is an awkward variant rarely programmed; bench dips are already in the library.
- Outside Leg Kick Push-Up: Gimmicky push-up with a sideways leg kick; not a standard gym exercise.
- Push-Up Inside Leg Kick: Gimmicky push-up with a leg kick; not a standard gym exercise.
- Rear Decline Bridge: Ambiguous name; the bridge variant cannot be identified with confidence.
- Seated Side Crunch (Wall): Obscure seated side-bend against a wall; oblique crunches and side bends already cover it.
- Standing Single Leg Curl: Bodyweight standing leg curl has no meaningful resistance (warm-up/rehab drill); the loaded Standing Leg Curl is already in the library.
- Superman Push-Up: Explosive push-up with a full-body flight phase; a plyometric/circus variant.
- Triceps Press: Generic name; the bodyweight movement it denotes cannot be identified with confidence.
- Weighted Round Arm: Vague source name; the movement cannot be identified with confidence.
- Drop Push Up: Drop push-up is a plyometric depth drill.
- Cable Rope Crossover Seated Row: Unclear crossed-rope seated row; cannot be identified as a distinct exercise and at best adds noise to the seated cable row.
- Cable Rope Extension Incline Bench Row: Unclear row-plus-extension combination; not a recognisable, programmable exercise.
- Cable Upper Row: Vague source name; the row variant cannot be identified with confidence.
- Back Pec Stretch: Vague name; source data mixes lats and chest and describes contradictory arm positions, so the stretch cannot be identified.
- Modified Push Up To Lower Arms: Unclear 'modified' variant of the forearm-to-hands push-up; the identifiable movement is wger-1406 (Plank-to-Elbow Extension).
- Twist Hip Lift: Not a recognisable standard exercise; a bridge with a lower-body twist that nobody programs as its own lift.
- Seated Wide Angle Pose Sequence: Yoga pose sequence; the static seated straddle stretch is already in the library (The Straddle).
- Dumbbell One Arm Reverse Grip Press: Ambiguous: name and source do not establish whether it is a bench or overhead press; too obscure to identify with confidence.
- Smith Machine Reverse Decline Close Grip Bench Press: Gimmicky stack of modifiers with 'reverse' undefined; the source describes the plain decline close-grip press (edb-1625).
- Cable Pulldown Bicep Curl: Unclear movement: the name suggests a pulldown and curl hybrid while the source describes pulling a bar down to the thighs.
- Cable Squatting Curl: Gimmicky squat-and-curl hybrid; not a standard gym exercise.
- Dumbbell Bicep Curl Lunge With Bowling Motion: Gimmicky combination (curl, lunge and bowling rotation) that nobody programs in a gym.
- Squat To Overhead Reach: Bodyweight warm-up/mobility drill (squat with an overhead reach), not a training exercise.
- Squat To Overhead Reach With Twist: Warm-up drill: bodyweight squat with overhead reach and torso twist.
- Posterior Step To Overhead Reach: Warm-up drill: unloaded reverse lunge with an overhead reach.
- Lunge With Twist: Bodyweight dynamic warm-up (lunge with torso rotation), not a loaded training exercise.
- Push And Pull Bodyweight: Vague name; the source describes only push-up steps, so no distinct exercise can be identified.
- Dumbbell Forward Lunge Triceps Extension: Gimmicky combo of a forward lunge and an overhead triceps extension; the light arm load leaves the lunge pointless.
- Dumbbell Tricep Kickback With Stork Stance: Single-leg (stork) stance only adds a balance gimmick to the standard dumbbell kickback.
- Bodyweight Side Lying Biceps Curl: No identifiable resistance; an unloaded side-lying elbow curl is not a trainable exercise.
- Biceps Leg Concentration Curl: Improvised curl using your own leg as resistance; not a gym exercise.
- Left Hook. Boxing: Boxing punch (sport drill), and a left-side-only variant.
- Spine Twist: Unloaded seated spinal rotation; a mobility/warm-up drill.
- Barbell Sitted Alternate Leg Raise: Unclear barbell variant of a seated alternating leg raise; cannot be identified with confidence and looks gimmicky.
- Barbell Sitted Alternate Leg Raise (Female): Female-model duplicate of an unclear barbell seated leg raise variant.
- Twisted Leg Raise (Female): Female-model copy of Twisted Leg Raise; adds only noise.
- Dumbbell Supported Squat: Dumbbell squat with a hand support only; near-identical noise variant of Dumbbell Squat.
- Dumbbell Single Leg Deadlift With Stepbox Support: Stepbox support adds nothing material; Dumbbell Single-Leg Romanian Deadlift covers it.
- Bench Pull-Ups: Ambiguous name (row under a bench or feet-on-bench pull-up); Inverted Row and Inverted Row On Bench already cover it.
- Potty Squat With Support: Supported version of a deep bodyweight squat; the hand support only adds noise.
- Bodyweight Standing Close-Grip One Arm Row: Close grip on a one-arm pole row is meaningless; noise variant of the single-arm standing row.
- Bodyweight Standing Close-Grip Row: Hand spacing on an upright post adds nothing; near-identical variant of Bodyweight Mid Row.
- Bodyweight Standing Row (With Towel): Towel looped around a post on a two-hand standing row; minor handle change that only adds noise to Bodyweight Mid Row.
- Bodyweight Squatting Row (With Towel): Towel copy of the squatting post row; adds noise.
- Side-To-Side Toe Touch (Male): Dynamic side-to-side toe touching is a warm-up drill.
- Arms Apart Circular Toe Touch (Male): Circular toe-touch is a warm-up drill.
- Hands Reversed Clasped Circular Toe Touch (Male): Circular toe-touch with clasped hands is a warm-up drill.
- Hands Clasped Circular Toe Touch (Male): Circular toe-touch with clasped hands is a warm-up drill.
- Half Knee Bends (Male): Half knee bends are a warm-up drill.
- Semi Squat Jump (Male): Plyometric jump; plyometrics are not imported.
- Hyght Dumbbell Fly: 'Hyght' is not a recognisable term; the variant cannot be identified with confidence.
- Elbow Dips: Niche calisthenics dip-to-forearm transition with an unclear name; not something to program in a gym.
- Impossible Dips: Gimmicky elite dip variant; not something to program in a gym.
- Weighted One Hand Pull Up: Weighted one-arm pull-up is an elite stunt; One Arm Chin-Up covers single-arm pulling.
- Stalder Press: Elite gymnastics press-to-handstand skill; not something to program in a gym.
- Elevator: 'Elevator' is not identifiable with confidence as a specific exercise.
- Straddle Maltese: Elite gymnastics Maltese skill; not something to program in a gym.
- Full Maltese: Elite gymnastics Maltese skill; not something to program in a gym.
- Swing 360: Bar swing trick, not a programmable gym exercise.
- Full Planche Push-Up: Elite gymnastics feat; Full Planche and the pseudo planche push-up cover planche training.
- Skater Hops: Lateral plyometric bounds; plyometrics are not imported.
- Quads: Name is just a muscle group; the exercise cannot be identified.
- Quick Feet V. 2: Agility footwork drill, and a 'V. 2' source variant.
- High Knee Against Wall: Sprint mechanics wall drill, not a gym exercise.
- Wheel Run: Running technique drill with an unclear name.
- Push To Run: Sprint start drill, not a gym exercise.
- Weighted Kneeling Step With Swing: Gimmicky kneeling-to-step swing combination nobody would program.
- Weighted Stretch Lunge: Vague name; the movement cannot be identified with confidence.
- Weighted Lunge With Swing: Gimmicky lunge-and-swing combination.
- Walking High Knees Lunge: Dynamic warm-up drill.
- Short Stride Run: Running technique drill; plain running is covered elsewhere.
- Power Point Plank: Unclear source name; the plank variant cannot be identified.
- Standing Archer: Unclear mobility drill; cannot be identified as a gym exercise.
- Ski Step: Lateral agility hop drill.
- Back And Forth Step: Agility stepping drill.

### Liftosaur: excluded on review (16)

- Barbell Cable Twist: Contradictory name (barbell cable twist); cannot be identified.
- Cable Twist: Contradictory name (cable twist with bodyweight); cannot be identified.
- Machine Cable Twist: Contradictory name mixing cable and machine; cannot be identified.
- Barbell Chest Fly: Not a real exercise; a chest fly cannot be done with a barbell.
- Crow Pose: Yoga arm-balance pose rather than a gym exercise.
- Deadlift: A deadlift with no load is not a meaningful exercise.
- Cable Hanging Leg Raise: Contradictory name; a hanging leg raise is not done on a cable.
- Hip Abductor: Bodyweight form of the seated machine abduction is not an identifiable exercise; side-lying versions exist.
- Hip Adductor: Bodyweight form of the seated machine adduction is not an identifiable exercise; side-lying version exists.
- Dumbbell Iso-Lateral Row: 'Iso-lateral' names a machine; the dumbbell version is too ambiguous to identify.
- Machine Lying Bicep Curl: Equipment permutation with no standard lying biceps curl machine; machine curls are covered by Machine Bicep Curl.
- Machine Pistol Squat: Not a real machine exercise; equipment permutation of the pistol squat (Smith Machine Pistol Squat already exists).
- Barbell Side Hip Abductor: No recognisable barbell side hip abduction; source equipment permutation.
- Barbell Single Leg Calf Raise: Impractical to balance a barbell on one leg for calf raises; single-leg calf raises are loaded with dumbbell, cable or machine.
- Barbell Sling Shot Bench Press: Requires a Sling Shot elastic band device, which the library excludes.
- Machine Squat: Too generic to identify which machine; specific squat machines (hack, V-squat, pendulum, Smith) are already in the library.
